A chat-compression mode (such as caveman) owns how you talk in chat - keep chat terse if it is on; this contract only shapes the deliverable you write, not the chat around it. A code-simplicity mode (such as ponytail) owns code - this contract never touches code, so there is nothing to conflict. Each mode keeps its own domain: terse chat, lazy code, human prose. No bold-for-emphasis inside prose sentences. No emojis in prose. Semicolons and parentheses where a writer would naturally use them.\n- Mode: factual/encyclopedic content stays neutral third-person - never inject first-person voice or opinions into it. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by running the app and observing behavior. Use when asked to verify a PR, confirm a fix works, test a change manually, check that a feature works, or validate local changes before pushing.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. CRITICAL!!: 
- NEVER EVER EVER create a branch!!! branches are FORBIDDEN!!!
- NEVER EVER EVER delete the `bugdrop-screenshots` branch, locally or remotely. It is protected project history.
- NEVER create a worktree, UNLESS the user has specifically asked for it!
- NEVER delete files unless the user has specifically asked for it. If you think a file is misplaced, unrelated, or stale, that is an entirely separate request — ask first. "Looks unrelated to this task" is not authorization to delete.

- When the user gives a UUID and asks what is going on with it, treat it as an OmniHarness conversation/session lookup: check `sqlite.db`, starting with the `runs` row for that UUID, then inspect related `workers`, `messages`, `execution_events`, queued messages, and validation/plan records as needed.
- To delete all conversations and associated persisted artifacts, use `scripts/delete-conversations.sh`

Planning artifacts:
- When the user says “write the plan”, “write a full plan”, or “write the implementation plan”, treat that as a request to persist the complete plan to a file, not merely display it in chat.
- If the user gives a path, write the plan there. Otherwise use `docs/superpowers/plans/YYYY-MM-DD-<kebab-case-feature-name>.md`, using the current date and a non-colliding filename.
- The saved file is the canonical full plan. The final response should link to it and summarize the result rather than replacing the file with a chat-only plan.
- This convention applies even when the current message omits the destination because “write the plan” is the project’s established shorthand for creating the plan artifact.
- If the same request explicitly says not to change the workspace or not to create a file, obey that more specific prohibition and ask for a destination when needed.

Testing:
- After any UI/frontend change, always run `pnpm build:interface:web` before considering the work complete.
- When testing the app, use the already-running process if one exists instead of starting another server.
- The normal runner URL is `http://localhost:3050`; the Vite development interface is usually at `http://localhost:5173`.
- Clean up any test sessions/conversations and their associated persisted artifacts before finishing.
- For lifecycle / chaos-style regressions (reconnect, restart, FK-on-delete, plan-review leftover state), use `pnpm test:lifecycle`. Scenarios live under `tests/lifecycle/scenarios/` and drive the control plane via HTTP/SSE — no Chromium. To debug a specific reported bug, mirror it as a new scenario file.
- Read `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md` before adding new server-side state transitions. It is the spec all new code is held to.
- Worker conversation content (direct-control and supervisor-driven worker turns) lives in the unified worker stream — one append-only JSONL per worker at `app-data/run-data/<runId>/<workerId>.jsonl`. Persist new worker content through `appendWorkerEntry` in `src/server/workers/output-store.ts`; render it through `WorkerEntriesManager` and the `entries` prop on `Terminal`. Do NOT add a parallel persistence layer (a sibling JSONL, a new `messages.kind`, an in-memory cache that the frontend reconciles against the stream). See `docs/architecture/worker-conversation-stream.md`.

Lifecycle observability rules (full doc: `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md`):
- Every server-side decision (spawn, reattach, recreate, give up, refuse, delete, fail) emits a typed named event via `emitNamedEvent` from `@/server/events/named-events`. Silent early returns and bare `catch {}` are bugs.
- User-relevant failures additionally emit `error.surfaced` with a stable `code` (typed union in `named-events.ts`), `surface`, and at least one relevant subject id such as `runId`/`workerId`/`conversationId`/`accountId`. Never funnel through a blanket wrapper in `api-errors.ts`.
- All SSE frames carry an `id:`. Clients reconnect with `Last-Event-ID`; the server replays from the ring buffer or emits `stream.resync_required`. Snapshot bootstrap is `GET /api/events?snapshot=1` (anchor id in the `x-omni-last-event-id` response header).
- Dev-only event log: `GET /api/events/log?since=<id>&runId=<id>` returns the ring buffer as JSON. Use this when triaging "X didn't happen" bug reports — if the event isn't there, the server didn't do the thing, and the next step is finding the silent branch.
- Chaos is a client-side concern. Never add fault-injection code paths to server code.

Frontend i18n:
- EVERY user-facing frontend string MUST live in `shared/locales/*.json` and be rendered with the `t()` function from `@/lib/i18n`.
- NEVER hardcode user-facing JSX text, button labels, dialog titles, aria-labels, titles, placeholders, empty states, status labels, help text, error fallback text, or visible option labels directly in components.
- For every new user-facing string, add the key to `shared/locales/en.json` and every other locale file in `shared/locales/` in the same change. Use stable dotted keys like `settings.runtime.recovery` or `conversation.sidebar.search`.
- Components that render translated strings and need to update when the language changes MUST subscribe to the i18n manager by calling `useI18nSnapshot()` from `@/lib/i18n`; then render text with the existing `t("key", params?)` function. The hook is only for re-rendering; `t()` is the translation API.
- Non-React modules that produce user-visible text must still use `t("key", params?)` at the boundary where text is produced. Protocol values, storage keys, API paths, CSS classes, model ids, and database values are not translation keys unless they are displayed as copy.
- Do not pass translated strings through transactions or persist translated UI copy. Store stable ids/values and translate at render time.

React Best Practices:
- MutableRefObject is deprecated in React; use RefObject instead.
- NEVER use require() to import modules. Use import instead.
- ALWAYS centralize all state in global single source of truth Manager classes. Components/listeners subscribe to updates from Managers and use Manager methods to update data.
- Always implement a custom data manager for any data structure. NEVER use separate state variables and arrays as the source of truth.
- NEVER send data around in transactions.
- Avoid using useEffect() to update state. Prefer useCallback() or Manager methods for explicit state transitions.
- Avoid callback hell, race conditions, and infinite loops.
- NEVER store string literals or UI settings for the frontend in the .env file. Use literals in files, inline arrays, or .json files instead.
- When debugging React: if a fix did not work, think hard about whether this is a race condition. Race conditions are common in React.

Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- sloptrim:sloptrim\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by running the app and observing behavior. Use when asked to verify a PR, confirm a fix works, test a change manually, check that a feature works, or validate local changes before pushing.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review\n- security-review",

1	export function normalizeProjectPath(projectPath: string) {
2	  return projectPath.trim().replace(/[/\\]+$/, "") || "/";
3	}
4	
5	function pathSeparatorFor(projectPath: string) {
6	  return projectPath.includes("\\") && !projectPath.includes("/") ? "\\" : "/";
7	}
8	
9	export function isSameOrDescendantPath(pathValue: string, rootValue: string) {
10	  const path = normalizeProjectPath(pathValue);
11	  const root = normalizeProjectPath(rootValue);
12	  if (path === root) {
13	    return true;
14	  }
15	
16	  return path.startsWith(`${root}${pathSeparatorFor(root)}`);
17	}
18	
19	export function normalizeExplicitProjectPaths(explicitProjects: string[]) {
20	  return Array.from(new Set(
21	    explicitProjects
22	      .map((projectPath) => normalizeProjectPath(projectPath))
23	      .filter((projectPath) => projectPath.length > 0),
24	  ));
25	}
26	
27	export function resolveStaleProjectFallback(
28	  explicitProjects: string[],
29	  storedProjectPaths: Array<string | null | undefined>,
30	) {
31	  const normalizedExplicitProjects = normalizeExplicitProjectPaths(explicitProjects);
32	  if (normalizedExplicitProjects.length === 0) {
33	    return null;
34	  }
35	
36	  const directRunCounts = new Map(normalizedExplicitProjects.map((projectPath) => [projectPath, 0]));
37	  let hasUnmatchedStoredPath = false;
38	
39	  for (const storedProjectPath of storedProjectPaths) {
40	    const storedPath = storedProjectPath?.trim()
41	      ? normalizeProjectPath(storedProjectPath)
42	      : null;
43	    if (!storedPath) {
44	      continue;
45	    }
46	
47	    const matchingExplicitProject = normalizedExplicitProjects.find((projectPath) => (
48	      isSameOrDescendantPath(storedPath, projectPath)
49	    ));
50	
51	    if (matchingExplicitProject) {
52	      directRunCounts.set(matchingExplicitProject, (directRunCounts.get(matchingExplicitProject) ?? 0) + 1);
53	    } else {
54	      hasUnmatchedStoredPath = true;
55	    }
56	  }
57	
58	  if (!hasUnmatchedStoredPath) {
59	    return null;
60	  }
61	
62	  const emptyExplicitProjects = normalizedExplicitProjects.filter((projectPath) => (
63	    (directRunCounts.get(projectPath) ?? 0) === 0
64	  ));
65	
66	  return emptyExplicitProjects.length === 1 ? emptyExplicitProjects[0] : null;
67	}
68	
69	export function resolveStoredProjectRoot(
70	  storedProjectPath: string | null | undefined,
71	  explicitProjects: string[],
72	  options: { staleFallbackProject?: string | null } = {},
73	) {
74	  const normalizedExplicitProjects = normalizeExplicitProjectPaths(explicitProjects);
75	  const storedPath = storedProjectPath?.trim()
76	    ? normalizeProjectPath(storedProjectPath)
77	    : null;
78	
79	  if (!storedPath) {
80	    return null;
81	  }
82	
83	  const matchingExplicitProject = normalizedExplicitProjects.find((projectPath) => (
84	    isSameOrDescendantPath(storedPath, projectPath)
85	  ));
86	  if (matchingExplicitProject) {
87	    return matchingExplicitProject;
88	  }
89	
90	  if (normalizedExplicitProjects.length === 0) {
91	    return storedPath;
92	  }
93	
94	  if (normalizedExplicitProjects.length === 1) {
95	    return normalizedExplicitProjects[0];
96	  }
97	
98	  if (options.staleFallbackProject) {
99	    return normalizeProjectPath(options.staleFallbackProject);
100	  }
101	
102	  return null;
103	}
104	

uuid: e76b611c-29b4-4971-9e86-fdab49771b2e
parent: 5ed35c2f-b770-4e67-96bd-c1b7b82f0e35

979	  } catch {
980	    return [];
981	  }
982	}

1	import type { HomeBootstrapPayload } from "@/shared/bootstrap";
2	import { busyMessageQueueManager } from "./BusyMessageQueueManager";
3	import { homeUiStateManager } from "./HomeUiStateManager";
4	import { planningReviewPreferencesManager } from "./PlanningReviewPreferencesManager";
5	import { settingsDraftManager } from "./SettingsDraftManager";
6	import { parseBrowserConversationRoute } from "./utils";
7	
8	let appliedHomeBootstrapId: string | null = null;
9	
10	export function applyHomeBootstrap(
11	  bootstrap: HomeBootstrapPayload | null | undefined,
12	  notify = true,
13	) {
14	  if (!bootstrap || appliedHomeBootstrapId === bootstrap.id) {
15	    return;
16	  }
17	
18	  appliedHomeBootstrapId = bootstrap.id;
19	  const browserRoute = typeof window === "undefined"
20	    ? bootstrap.route
21	    : parseBrowserConversationRoute(window.location);
22	  const routeSelectedRunId = browserRoute.selectedRunId;
23	  const settingsValues = bootstrap.initialQueries.settings?.values ?? {};
24	
25	  if (bootstrap.initialQueries.settings) {
26	    settingsDraftManager.hydrate(settingsValues, notify);
27	    planningReviewPreferencesManager.hydrate(settingsValues);
28	  }
29	
30	  homeUiStateManager.patch((current) => ({
31	    routeReady: true,
32	    hasReceivedInitialEventStreamPayload: Boolean(bootstrap.initialEventState),
33	    selectedRunId: routeSelectedRunId,
34	    draftProjectPath: routeSelectedRunId ? null : browserRoute.draftProjectPath,
35	    pairTokenFromUrl: browserRoute.pairTokenFromUrl,
36	    apiKeys: { ...current.apiKeys, ...settingsValues },
37	    settingsDiagnostics: bootstrap.initialQueries.settings?.diagnostics ?? current.settingsDiagnostics,
38	  }), notify);
39	
40	  const initialSnapshotRunId = bootstrap.initialEventState?.snapshotRunId?.trim();
41	  if (bootstrap.initialEventState?.queuedMessages && initialSnapshotRunId) {
42	    busyMessageQueueManager.setQueuedMessages(bootstrap.initialEventState.queuedMessages, {
43	      runId: initialSnapshotRunId,
44	      notify,
45	    });
46	  }
47	}
48

4	function readSearchParams(request: Request) {
5	  const url = new URL(request.url);

7	
8	  for (const key of url.searchParams.keys()) {

1	import { buildAuthSessionState } from "@/server/auth/session-state";
2	import { buildPersistedEventPayload } from "@/server/events/persisted-snapshot";
3	import { getEventStreamCursor } from "@/server/events/named-events";
4	import { readSettingsState } from "@/server/settings/read";
5	import { buildRunnerBootstrapIdentity } from "@/server/runner/identity";
6	import type { HomeBootstrapPayload } from "@/shared/bootstrap";
7	
8	export type { HomeBootstrapPayload } from "@/shared/bootstrap";
9	
10	type PageSearchParams = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
11	
12	export type RuntimeBootstrapOptions = {
13	  searchParams?: PageSearchParams;
14	  requestHeaders: Headers;
15	  includeInitialData?: boolean;
16	};
17	
18	function firstParam(value: string | string[] | undefined) {
19	  return Array.isArray(value) ? value[0] ?? "" : value ?? "";
20	}
21	
22	function normalizeParam(value: string | string[] | undefined) {
23	  return firstParam(value).trim() || null;
24	}
25	
26	function buildRequestUrl(searchParams: PageSearchParams, requestHeaders: Headers) {
27	  const host = requestHeaders.get("x-forwarded-host")?.split(",")[0]?.trim()
28	    || requestHeaders.get("host")?.trim()
29	    || "localhost";
30	  const protocol = requestHeaders.get("x-forwarded-proto")?.split(",")[0]?.trim() || "http";
31	  const url = new URL(`${protocol}://${host}/`);
32	
33	  for (const [key, rawValue] of Object.entries(searchParams)) {
34	    const values = Array.isArray(rawValue) ? rawValue : [rawValue];
35	    for (const value of values) {
36	      if (value != null) {
37	        url.searchParams.append(key, value);
38	      }
39	    }
40	  }
41	
42	  return url;
43	}
44	
45	export async function buildRuntimeBootstrap({
46	  searchParams = {},
47	  requestHeaders,
48	  includeInitialData = true,
49	}: RuntimeBootstrapOptions): Promise<HomeBootstrapPayload> {
50	  const requestUrl = buildRequestUrl(searchParams, requestHeaders);
51	  const selectedRunId = normalizeParam(searchParams.run);
52	  const draftProjectPath = normalizeParam(searchParams.project);
53	  const pairTokenFromUrl = normalizeParam(searchParams.pair);
54	
55	  const session = await buildAuthSessionState({
56	    url: requestUrl.toString(),
57	    headers: requestHeaders,
58	  });
59	  const appUnlocked = Boolean(session && (!session.enabled || session.authenticated));
60	
61	  const [runner, [settings, initialEventState]] = await Promise.all([
62	    buildRunnerBootstrapIdentity(),
63	    appUnlocked && includeInitialData
64	      ? Promise.all([
65	        readSettingsState(),
66	        buildPersistedEventPayload({ selectedRunId }),
67	      ])
68	      : Promise.resolve([null, null] as const),
69	  ]);
70	
71	  return {
72	    id: JSON.stringify({
73	      builtAt: Date.now(),
74	      selectedRunId,
75	      draftProjectPath,
76	      pairTokenFromUrl,
77	      sessionAuthenticated: session?.authenticated ?? false,
78	      eventRunCount: initialEventState?.runs?.length ?? 0,
79	    }),
80	    route: {
81	      selectedRunId,
82	      draftProjectPath,
83	      pairTokenFromUrl,
84	    },
85	    initialEventState,
86	    initialLastEventId: getEventStreamCursor(),
87	    initialQueries: {
88	      session,
89	      settings,
90	    },
91	    features: {
92	      // The unified worker stream is unconditionally enabled after the
93	      // Phase 5 cutover. The boolean is kept on the bootstrap shape so
94	      // older client/server pairs don't break during the rollout
95	      // window; future cleanup may drop it entirely.
96	      unifiedWorkerStream: true,
97	    },
98	    runner,
99	  };
100	}

1	import { db } from "@/server/db";
2	import { settings } from "@/server/db/schema";
3	import { buildAppError } from "@/server/api-errors";
4	import { decryptSettingValue, shouldEncryptSetting } from "@/server/settings/crypto";
5	import { canonicalizePersistedProjectRoots } from "@/server/projects/canonicalize";
6	import { readSystemResourceSnapshot } from "@/server/agent-runtime/resource-admission";
7	import type { SettingsResponse } from "@/shared/home-types";
8	
9	function isInternalSettingKey(key: string) {
10	  return key.startsWith("__");
11	}
12	
13	function buildSecretPreview(value: string): string {
14	  const trimmed = value.trim();
15	  if (!trimmed) return "";
16	  if (trimmed.length <= 8) {
17	    return "•".repeat(Math.max(4, trimmed.length));
18	  }
19	  return `${trimmed.slice(0, 4)}••••${trimmed.slice(-4)}`;
20	}
21	
22	function normalizeResourceSnapshot(snapshot: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof readSystemResourceSnapshot>>): NonNullable<SettingsResponse["resourceSnapshot"]> {
23	  return {
24	    memoryFreePercent: snapshot.memoryFreePercent ?? null,
25	    totalMemoryMb: snapshot.totalMemoryMb ?? null,
26	    diskFreeMb: snapshot.diskFreeMb ?? null,
27	    diskTotalMb: snapshot.diskTotalMb ?? null,
28	  };
29	}
30	
31	export async function readSettingsState(): Promise<SettingsResponse> {
32	  const allSettings = await db.select().from(settings);
33	  const projectSetting = allSettings.find((setting) => setting.key === "PROJECTS");
34	  if (projectSetting) {
35	    await canonicalizePersistedProjectRoots(projectSetting.value);
36	  }
37	  const diagnostics: ReturnType<typeof buildAppError>[] = [];
38	  const values = Object.fromEntries(allSettings.flatMap((setting) => {
39	    if (isInternalSettingKey(setting.key)) {
40	      return [];
41	    }
42	
43	    if (!shouldEncryptSetting(setting.key)) {
44	      return [[setting.key, setting.value]];
45	    }
46	
47	    return [];
48	  }));
49	  const secrets = Object.fromEntries(allSettings.flatMap((setting) => {
50	    if (isInternalSettingKey(setting.key)) {
51	      return [];
52	    }
53	
54	    if (!shouldEncryptSetting(setting.key)) {
55	      return [];
56	    }
57	
58	    const configured = setting.value.trim().length > 0;
59	    let preview: string | undefined;
60	    if (configured) {
61	      try {
62	        preview = buildSecretPreview(decryptSettingValue(setting.value));
63	      } catch {
64	        preview = undefined;
65	      }
66	    }
67	
68	    return [[setting.key, {
69	      configured,
70	      updatedAt: new Date(setting.updatedAt).toISOString(),
71	      preview,
72	    }]];
73	  }));
74	
75	  const resourceSnapshot = normalizeResourceSnapshot(await readSystemResourceSnapshot(process.cwd()));
76	
77	  return { values, secrets, diagnostics, resourceSnapshot };
78	}
79

1	import fs from "fs";
2	import path from "path";
3	import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
4	import { normalizeExplicitProjectPaths, isSameOrDescendantPath, resolveStaleProjectFallback, resolveStoredProjectRoot } from "@/lib/project-paths";
5	import { db } from "@/server/db";
6	import { runs, workers } from "@/server/db/schema";
7	
8	function parseProjectSetting(value: string | null | undefined) {
9	  if (!value?.trim()) {
10	    return [];
11	  }
12	
13	  try {
14	    const parsed = JSON.parse(value);
15	    return Array.isArray(parsed)
16	      ? parsed.filter((entry): entry is string => typeof entry === "string")
17	      : [];
18	  } catch {
19	    return [];
20	  }
21	}
22	
23	function rebasePath(pathValue: string, oldRoot: string, newRoot: string) {
24	  if (!isSameOrDescendantPath(pathValue, oldRoot)) {
25	    return pathValue;
26	  }
27	
28	  const relativePath = path.relative(oldRoot, pathValue);
29	  if (!relativePath) {
30	    return newRoot;
31	  }
32	
33	  if (relativePath.startsWith("..") || path.isAbsolute(relativePath)) {
34	    return pathValue;
35	  }
36	
37	  return path.join(newRoot, relativePath);
38	}
39	
40	export async function canonicalizePersistedProjectRoots(projectSettingValue: string | null | undefined) {
41	  const explicitProjects = normalizeExplicitProjectPaths(parseProjectSetting(projectSettingValue));
42	  if (explicitProjects.length === 0) {
43	    return;
44	  }
45	
46	  const allRuns = await db.select().from(runs);
47	  const allWorkers = await db.select().from(workers);
48	  const staleFallbackProject = resolveStaleProjectFallback(
49	    explicitProjects,
50	    allRuns.map((run) => run.projectPath),
51	  );
52	
53	  for (const run of allRuns) {
54	    const storedProjectPath = run.projectPath?.trim();
55	    const resolvedProjectRoot = resolveStoredProjectRoot(storedProjectPath, explicitProjects, { staleFallbackProject });
56	    if (!storedProjectPath || !resolvedProjectRoot) {
57	      continue;
58	    }
59	
60	    const projectRoot = path.resolve(resolvedProjectRoot);
61	    if (isSameOrDescendantPath(storedProjectPath, projectRoot)) {
62	      continue;
63	    }
64	
65	    const oldRoot = path.resolve(storedProjectPath);
66	    if (fs.existsSync(oldRoot)) {
67	      continue;
68	    }
69	
70	    await db.update(runs).set({
71	      projectPath: projectRoot,
72	      updatedAt: new Date(),
73	    }).where(eq(runs.id, run.id));
74	
75	    for (const worker of allWorkers.filter((candidate) => candidate.runId === run.id)) {
76	      const nextCwd = rebasePath(path.resolve(worker.cwd), oldRoot, projectRoot);
77	      if (nextCwd === worker.cwd) {
78	        continue;
79	      }
80	
81	      await db.update(workers).set({
82	        cwd: nextCwd,
83	        updatedAt: new Date(),
84	      }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
85	    }
86	  }
87	}
88

1	import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
2	import { db } from "@/server/db";
3	import { settings } from "@/server/db/schema";
4	import { buildAppError, errorResponse } from "@/server/api-errors";
5	import { requireApiSession } from "@/server/auth/guards";
6	import { canonicalizePersistedProjectRoots } from "@/server/projects/canonicalize";
7	import { encryptSettingValue, shouldEncryptSetting } from "@/server/settings/crypto";
8	import { updateRuntimeSettings } from "@/server/bridge-client";
9	import { emitNamedEvent } from "@/server/events/named-events";
10	import { readSystemResourceSnapshot } from "@/server/agent-runtime/resource-admission";
11	import { RUNTIME_RESOURCE_SETTING_KEYS } from "@/lib/runtime-resource-settings";
12	import {
13	  CLAUDE_MODEL_GATEWAY_SETTING_KEYS,
14	  normalizeClaudeGatewayBaseUrl,
15	  validateClaudeGatewayModel,
16	} from "@/lib/claude-model-gateway";
17	import { validateCommitWorkerSettings } from "@/lib/commit-workflow";
18	import { readClaudeModelGatewaySettings } from "@/server/integrations/claude-model-gateway/settings";
19	import type { OmniHttpHandler } from "@/runtime/http/registry";
20	
21	function isInternalSettingKey(key: string) {
22	  return key.startsWith("__");
23	}
24	
25	function describeError(error: unknown) {
26	  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
27	}
28	
29	async function validateClaudeGatewayDraft(body: Record<string, unknown>) {
30	  const gatewayKeys = new Set<string>(Object.values(CLAUDE_MODEL_GATEWAY_SETTING_KEYS));
31	  if (!Object.keys(body).some((key) => gatewayKeys.has(key))) return;
32	  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(body)) {
33	    if (gatewayKeys.has(key) && typeof value !== "string") throw new Error(`Gateway setting ${key} must be a string.`);
34	  }
35	  const current = await readClaudeModelGatewaySettings();
36	  const mode = body[CLAUDE_MODEL_GATEWAY_SETTING_KEYS.mode] ?? current.mode;
37	  if (mode !== "managed" && mode !== "external") throw new Error("Invalid Claude model gateway mode.");
38	  const enabled = body[CLAUDE_MODEL_GATEWAY_SETTING_KEYS.enabled];
39	  if (enabled !== undefined && enabled !== "true" && enabled !== "false") {
40	    throw new Error("Claude model gateway enabled setting must be true or false.");
41	  }
42	  const baseUrl = body[CLAUDE_MODEL_GATEWAY_SETTING_KEYS.baseUrl];
43	  normalizeClaudeGatewayBaseUrl(typeof baseUrl === "string" ? baseUrl : current.baseUrl);
44	  const rawModels = body[CLAUDE_MODEL_GATEWAY_SETTING_KEYS.models];
45	  if (typeof rawModels === "string") {
46	    const parsed = JSON.parse(rawModels) as unknown;
47	    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) throw new Error("Claude model gateway models must be an array.");
48	    parsed.forEach((entry) => {
49	      if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object" || Array.isArray(entry)) throw new Error("Claude model gateway models must contain objects.");
50	      const model = entry as { id?: unknown; label?: unknown };
51	      if (typeof model.id !== "string" || (model.label != null && typeof model.label !== "string")) {
52	        throw new Error("Claude model gateway model fields are invalid.");
53	      }
54	      validateClaudeGatewayModel({ id: model.id, ...(typeof model.label === "string" ? { label: model.label } : {}) });
55	    });
56	  }
57	}
58	
59	async function getSettings(request: Request) {
60	  const auth = await requireApiSession(request, {
61	    source: "Settings",
62	    action: "Load saved settings",
63	  });
64	  if (auth.response) {
65	    return auth.response;
66	  }
67	
68	  const allSettings = await db.select().from(settings);
69	  // canonicalizePersistedProjectRoots is a one-shot fixup over all runs +
70	  // workers that does sync fs.existsSync and serial db.update per drifted
71	  // row. Running it on every GET (which is polled by React Query and SSE
72	  // reconnects) was costing 30-60s with a large run history and saturating
73	  // the event loop. Canonicalization only matters when PROJECTS changes —
74	  // which happens on POST.
75	  const diagnostics: ReturnType<typeof buildAppError>[] = [];
76	  const values = Object.fromEntries(allSettings.flatMap((setting) => {
77	    if (isInternalSettingKey(setting.key)) {
78	      return [];
79	    }
80	
81	    if (!shouldEncryptSetting(setting.key)) {
82	      return [[setting.key, setting.value]];
83	    }
84	
85	    return [];
86	  }));
87	  const secrets = Object.fromEntries(allSettings.flatMap((setting) => {
88	    if (isInternalSettingKey(setting.key)) {
89	      return [];
90	    }
91	
92	    if (!shouldEncryptSetting(setting.key)) {
93	      return [];
94	    }
95	
96	    return [[setting.key, {
97	      configured: setting.value.trim().length > 0,
98	      updatedAt: new Date(setting.updatedAt).toISOString(),
99	    }]];
100	  }));
101	
102	  const resourceSnapshot = await readSystemResourceSnapshot(process.cwd());
103	
104	  return Response.json({ values, secrets, diagnostics, resourceSnapshot });
105	}
106	
107	async function postSettings(request: Request) {
108	  const auth = await requireApiSession(request, {
109	    source: "Settings",
110	    action: "Save settings",
111	    enforceSameOrigin: true,
112	  });
113	  if (auth.response) {
114	    return auth.response;
115	  }
116	
117	  const body = await request.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
118	  try {
119	    validateCommitWorkerSettings(body);
120	  } catch (error) {
121	    return Response.json({ error: { code: "invalid_commit_worker_settings", message: describeError(error) } }, { status: 400 });
122	  }
123	  try {
124	    await validateClaudeGatewayDraft(body);
125	  } catch (error) {
126	    return Response.json({ error: { code: "invalid_gateway_settings", message: describeError(error) } }, { status: 400 });
127	  }
128	  let projectSettingValue: string | null = null;
129	  const runtimeResourceSettings: Record<string, string> = {};
130	  const runtimeResourceKeys = new Set<string>(Object.values(RUNTIME_RESOURCE_SETTING_KEYS));
131	  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(body)) {
132	    if (isInternalSettingKey(key)) {
133	      continue;
134	    }
135	
136	    if (typeof value === "string") {
137	      if (key === "PROJECTS") {
138	        projectSettingValue = value;
139	      }
140	      if (runtimeResourceKeys.has(key)) {
141	        runtimeResourceSettings[key] = value;
142	      }
143	      const isSecret = shouldEncryptSetting(key);
144	      if (isSecret && value.trim() === "") {
145	        const existing = await db.select().from(settings).where(eq(settings.key, key)).get();
146	        if (existing) {
147	          continue;
148	        }
149	      }
150	
151	      const storedValue = isSecret ? encryptSettingValue(value) : value;
152	      await db.insert(settings)
153	        .values({ key, value: storedValue, updatedAt: new Date() })
154	        .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: settings.key, set: { value: storedValue, updatedAt: new Date() } });
155	    }
156	  }
157	  if (projectSettingValue !== null) {
158	    await canonicalizePersistedProjectRoots(projectSettingValue);
159	  }
160	  if (Object.keys(runtimeResourceSettings).length > 0) {
161	    try {
162	      await updateRuntimeSettings(runtimeResourceSettings);
163	    } catch (error) {
164	      const keys = Object.keys(runtimeResourceSettings);
165	      const reason = describeError(error);
166	      emitNamedEvent({ kind: "runtime.settings_apply_failed", keys, reason });
167	      emitNamedEvent({
168	        kind: "error.surfaced",
169	        code: "runtime.settings_apply_failed",
170	        message: `Runtime settings were saved, but the running agent runtime did not apply them yet: ${reason}`,
171	        surface: "log",
172	      });
173	    }
174	  }
175	  return Response.json({ ok: true });
176	}
177	
178	export const handleSettingsRequest: OmniHttpHandler = async (request) => {
179	  try {
180	    if (request.method === "GET") {
181	      return getSettings(request);
182	    }
183	    if (request.method === "POST") {
184	      return postSettings(request);
185	    }
186	    return Response.json({ error: { code: "method_not_allowed", message: "Method not allowed." } }, {
187	      status: 405,
188	      headers: { allow: "GET, POST" },
189	    });
190	  } catch (error) {
191	    return errorResponse(error, {
192	      status: 500,
193	      source: "Settings",
194	      action: request.method === "POST" ? "Save settings" : "Load saved settings",
195	    });
196	  }
197	};

1	import { useEffect } from "react";
2	import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

10	interface ProjectMemorySettingsPanelProps {
11	  projectPath: string | null;
12	  globalMemoryEnabled: boolean;
13	}
14	
15	function formatBytes(size: number) {
16	  if (size < 1024) {
17	    return `${size} B`;
18	  }
19	  return `${(size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
20	}
21	
22	export function ProjectMemorySettingsPanel({ projectPath, globalMemoryEnabled }: ProjectMemorySettingsPanelProps) {
23	  useI18nSnapshot();
24	  const runtimeApis = useRuntimeAPIs();
25	  projectMemoryPanelManager.configure(runtimeApis.settings.projectMemory);
26	  const state = useManagerSnapshot(projectMemoryPanelManager);
27	
28	  useEffect(() => {
29	    projectMemoryPanelManager.setProjectPath(projectPath);
30	    void projectMemoryPanelManager.reloadList();
31	  }, [projectPath, runtimeApis.settings.projectMemory]);
32	
33	  useEffect(() => {
34	    if (state.projectPath && state.selectedPath) {
35	      void projectMemoryPanelManager.loadFile();
36	    }
37	  }, [state.projectPath, state.selectedPath]);
38	
39	  const dirty = state.content !== state.originalContent;
40	  const effectiveEnabled = globalMemoryEnabled && state.enabled;
41	
42	  if (!projectPath) {
43	    return (
44	      <div className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-4">
45	        <div className="text-sm font-medium">{t("settings.memory.title")}</div>
46	        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{t("settings.memory.noProject")}</p>
47	      </div>
48	    );
49	  }
50	
51	  return (
52	    <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-4">
53	      <div className="space-y-1">
54	        <div className="text-sm font-medium">{t("settings.memory.title")}</div>
55	        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{t("settings.memory.description")}</p>
56	        <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground/80">
57	          {t("settings.memory.projectPathLabel")}: <span className="font-mono">{projectPath}</span>
58	        </p>
59	      </div>
60	
61	      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/70 p-3">
62	        <Switch
63	          id="PROJECT_MEMORY_ENABLED"
64	          aria-label={t("settings.memory.toggleEnabled")}
65	          checked={effectiveEnabled}
66	          disabled={!globalMemoryEnabled}
67	          onCheckedChange={(checked) => void projectMemoryPanelManager.toggleEnabled(checked)}
68	        />
69	        <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1">
70	          <div className="text-sm font-medium">{t("settings.memory.enabledForProject")}</div>
71	          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
72	            {!globalMemoryEnabled
73	              ? t("settings.memory.disabledGlobally")
74	              : state.enabled
75	                ? t("settings.memory.enabledHelp")
76	                : t("settings.memory.disabledForProject")}
77	          </p>
78	        </div>
79	      </div>
80	
81	      <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-[200px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
82	        <div className="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/70 p-2">
83	          {state.files.length === 0 ? (
84	            <p className="px-2 py-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{t("settings.memory.empty")}</p>
85	          ) : (
86	            <ul className="space-y-1">
87	              {state.files.map((file) => (
88	                <li key={file.path}>
89	                  <button
90	                    type="button"
91	                    onClick={() => void projectMemoryPanelManager.selectPath(file.path)}
92	                    className={cn(
93	                      "w-full rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-xs transition-colors",
94	                      state.selectedPath === file.path
95	                        ? "bg-muted text-foreground"
96	                        : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted/60 hover:text-foreground",
97	                    )}
98	                  >
99	                    <div className="truncate font-mono">{file.path}</div>
100	                    <div className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground/70">{formatBytes(file.size)}</div>
101	                  </button>
102	                </li>
103	              ))}
104	            </ul>
105	          )}
106	        </div>
107	
108	        <div className="flex min-h-[280px] flex-col gap-2">
109	          {state.selectedPath ? (
110	            <>
111	              <textarea
112	                className="flex-1 w-full resize-none rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/70 p-3 font-mono text-xs"
113	                value={state.content}
114	                onChange={(event) => projectMemoryPanelManager.setContent(event.target.value)}
115	                spellCheck={false}
116	                disabled={state.loading || state.saving}
117	              />
118	              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
119	                <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
120	                  {state.saveStatus === "saved"
121	                    ? t("settings.memory.saved")
122	                    : dirty
123	                      ? t("settings.memory.unsaved")
124	                      : ""}
125	                </span>
126	                <Button
127	                  size="sm"
128	                  onClick={() => void projectMemoryPanelManager.save()}
129	                  disabled={!dirty || state.saving}
130	                >
131	                  {state.saving ? t("settings.memory.saving") : t("settings.memory.save")}
132	                </Button>
133	              </div>
134	            </>
135	          ) : (
136	            <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
137	              {state.loading ? t("settings.memory.loading") : t("settings.memory.selectFile")}
138	            </div>
139	          )}
140	        </div>
141	      </div>
142	
143	      {state.error ? (
144	        <p className="text-xs text-destructive">{state.error}</p>
145	      ) : null}
146	    </div>
147	  );
148	}

33	
34	interface SettingsDialogProps {
35	  open: boolean;
36	  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
37	  activeSettingsTab: SettingsTab;
38	  setActiveSettingsTab: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<SettingsTab>>;
39	  activeLlmProfileTab: LlmProfileTab;
40	  setActiveLlmProfileTab: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<LlmProfileTab>>;
41	  settingsDraft: SettingsDraftState;
42	  setSetting: (key: string, value: string) => void;

68	export function SettingsDialog({
69	  open,
70	  onOpenChange,
71	  activeSettingsTab,
72	  setActiveSettingsTab,
73	  activeLlmProfileTab,
74	  setActiveLlmProfileTab,
75	  settingsDraft,
76	  setSetting,
77	  discardSettingsDraft,
78	  secretStates,
79	  settingsWorkers,
80	  accounts,
81	  onAccountsChanged,
82	  onRefreshAccounts,
83	  workerCatalogQuery,
84	  onRefreshWorkerCatalog,
85	  workerCatalogRefreshing,
86	  settingsDiagnostics,
87	  resourceSnapshot,
88	  saveSettings,
89	  activeProjectPath,
90	  claudeAccountAuthManager,
91	}: SettingsDialogProps) {
92	  const appearancePreferences = useManagerSnapshot(appearancePreferencesManager);
93	  useI18nSnapshot();
94	  const serverSettingsDirty = settingsDraft.dirtyKeys.size > 0;
95	  const localPreferencesDirty = appearancePreferences.dirtyKeys.size > 0;
96	  const isDirty = serverSettingsDirty || localPreferencesDirty;
97	  const handleOpenChange = (nextOpen: boolean) => {
98	    if (!nextOpen) {
99	      discardSettingsDraft();

101	    }
102	    onOpenChange(nextOpen);
103	  };

107	
108	    if (isServerDirty) {
109	      saveSettings.mutate();
110	      return;
111	    }
112	
113	    appearancePreferencesManager.saveDraft();
114	    handleOpenChange(false);
115	  };
116	
117	  return (
118	    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={handleOpenChange}>
119	      <DialogContent className="flex max-h-[min(760px,calc(100dvh-2rem))] flex-col overflow-hidden sm:max-h-[min(760px,calc(100dvh-3rem))] sm:max-w-2xl">
120	        <DialogHeader className="shrink-0 pr-8">

122	        </DialogHeader>
123	
124	        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
125	          <div className="space-y-4">
126	            <div className="inline-flex flex-wrap rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/30 p-1">
127	              {SETTINGS_TABS.map((tab) => (
128	                <button
129	                  key={tab.value}
130	                  type="button"
131	                  className={cn(
132	                    "rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors",
133	                    activeSettingsTab === tab.value
134	                      ? "bg-background text-foreground shadow-sm"
135	                      : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground",
136	                  )}
137	                  aria-pressed={activeSettingsTab === tab.value}
138	                  onClick={() => setActiveSettingsTab(tab.value)}
139	                >
140	                  {t(tab.labelKey)}
141	                </button>
142	              ))}
143	            </div>
144	
145	            {activeSettingsTab === "general" ? (
146	              <GeneralSettingsPanel
147	                settings={settingsDraft.draft}
148	                setSetting={setSetting}
149	                workerModels={workerCatalogQuery.data?.workerModels}
150	              />
151	            ) : null}
152	            {activeSettingsTab === "models" ? (
153	              <ModelsSettingsPanel
154	                activeLlmProfileTab={activeLlmProfileTab}
155	                setActiveLlmProfileTab={setActiveLlmProfileTab}
156	                settings={settingsDraft.draft}
157	                setSetting={setSetting}
158	                secretStates={secretStates}
159	              />
160	            ) : null}
161	            {activeSettingsTab === "credentials" ? (
162	              <CredentialsSettingsPanel
163	                settings={settingsDraft.draft}
164	                setSetting={setSetting}
165	              />
166	            ) : null}
167	            {activeSettingsTab === "agents" ? (
168	              <AgentsSettingsPanel
169	                settings={settingsDraft.draft}
170	                setSetting={setSetting}
171	                settingsWorkers={settingsWorkers}
172	                accounts={accounts}
173	                onAccountsChanged={onAccountsChanged}
174	                onRefreshAccounts={onRefreshAccounts}
175	                workerModels={workerCatalogQuery.data?.workerModels}
176	                workerModelsRefreshing={workerCatalogQuery.data?.workerModelsRefreshing}
177	                workerCatalogQuery={workerCatalogQuery}
178	                onRefreshWorkerCatalog={onRefreshWorkerCatalog}
179	                workerCatalogRefreshing={workerCatalogRefreshing}
180	                secretStates={secretStates}
181	                settingsDirtyKeys={settingsDraft.dirtyKeys}
182	                claudeAccountAuthManager={claudeAccountAuthManager}
183	              />
184	            ) : null}
185	            {activeSettingsTab === "runtime" ? (
186	              <RuntimeSettingsPanel
187	                settings={settingsDraft.draft}
188	                setSetting={setSetting}
189	                resourceSnapshot={resourceSnapshot}
190	              />
191	            ) : null}

194	                projectPath={activeProjectPath}
195	                globalMemoryEnabled={parseBooleanSetting(settingsDraft.draft.SUPERVISOR_MEMORY_ENABLED, true)}
196	              />
197	            ) : null}
198	
199	            {settingsDiagnostics.length > 0 ? (

201	                {settingsDiagnostics.map((error) => (

203	                ))}
204	              </div>
205	            ) : null}
206

210	                  <ErrorNotice key={appErrorKey(error)} error={error} />
211	                ))}
212	              </div>
213	            ) : null}
214	
215	            {saveSettings.error ? (

217	                error={buildInlineError(saveSettings.error, {

221	              />
222	            ) : null}
223	          </div>
224	        </div>
225

232	            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={handleCancel}>{t("common.cancel")}</Button>
233	            <Button onClick={handleSave} disabled={saveSettings.isPending || !isDirty}>

235	            </Button>
236	          </div>
237	        </DialogFooter>
238	      </DialogContent>
239	    </Dialog>

5	  GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_EFFORT_SETTING,
6	  GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_MODEL_SETTING,
7	  GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_TYPE_SETTING,
8	  normalizeCommitWorkerSettings,
9	} from "@/lib/commit-workflow";
10	import { getWorkerModelOptions } from "@/interface/home/utils";
11	import { WORKER_OPTIONS } from "@/interface/home/constants";
12	import type { WorkerModelCatalog } from "@/interface/home/types";
13	import { t, useI18nSnapshot } from "@/lib/i18n";
14	import { AppearanceSettingsPanel } from "./AppearanceSettingsPanel";
15	import { NotificationsSettingsPanel } from "./NotificationsSettingsPanel";
16	
17	interface GeneralSettingsPanelProps {
18	  settings: Record<string, string>;
19	  setSetting: (key: string, value: string) => void;
20	  workerModels?: Partial<WorkerModelCatalog>;
21	}
22	
23	function workerLabelKey(workerType: string) {
24	  return `settings.commitAgent.cli.${workerType}`;
25	}
26	
27	function effortLabelKey(effort: string) {
28	  return `settings.commitAgent.effort.${effort.replace(" ", "")}`;
29	}
30	
31	export function GeneralSettingsPanel({ settings, setSetting, workerModels }: GeneralSettingsPanelProps) {
32	  useI18nSnapshot();
33	  const commitWorker = normalizeCommitWorkerSettings(settings);
34	  const modelOptions = getWorkerModelOptions(workerModels, commitWorker.workerType);
35	  const visibleModelOptions = modelOptions.some((option) => option.value === commitWorker.model)
36	    ? modelOptions
37	    : [{ value: commitWorker.model, label: commitWorker.model }, ...modelOptions];
38	  const workerOptions = WORKER_OPTIONS.map((option) => ({
39	    value: option.value,
40	    label: t(workerLabelKey(option.value)),
41	  }));
42	  const effortOptions = COMMIT_WORKER_EFFORTS.map((effort) => ({
43	    value: effort,
44	    label: t(effortLabelKey(effort)),
45	  }));
46	
47	  const handleWorkerTypeChange = (workerType: string) => {
48	    setSetting(GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_TYPE_SETTING, workerType);
49	    const nextModel = getWorkerModelOptions(workerModels, workerType as keyof WorkerModelCatalog)[0]?.value;
50	    if (nextModel) {
51	      setSetting(GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_MODEL_SETTING, nextModel);
52	    }
53	  };
54	
55	  return (
56	    <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-4">
57	      <LanguageSelect />
58	      <AppearanceSettingsPanel />
59	      <NotificationsSettingsPanel />
60	      <div className="space-y-3">
61	        <div className="space-y-1">
62	          <div className="text-sm font-semibold">{t("settings.commitAgent.title")}</div>
63	          <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{t("settings.commitAgent.description")}</p>
64	        </div>
65	        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(9rem,0.9fr)_minmax(14rem,1.35fr)] sm:items-center">
66	          <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground" htmlFor={GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_TYPE_SETTING}>
67	            {t("settings.commitAgent.cli")}
68	          </label>
69	          <Select
70	            id={GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_TYPE_SETTING}
71	            ariaLabel={t("settings.commitAgent.cli")}
72	            value={commitWorker.workerType}
73	            options={workerOptions}
74	            onValueChange={handleWorkerTypeChange}
75	          />
76	          <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground" htmlFor={GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_MODEL_SETTING}>
77	            {t("settings.commitAgent.model")}
78	          </label>
79	          <Select
80	            id={GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_MODEL_SETTING}
81	            ariaLabel={t("settings.commitAgent.model")}
82	            value={commitWorker.model}
83	            options={visibleModelOptions}
84	            native
85	            onValueChange={(value) => setSetting(GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_MODEL_SETTING, value)}
86	          />
87	          <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground" htmlFor={GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_EFFORT_SETTING}>
88	            {t("settings.commitAgent.effort")}
89	          </label>
90	          <Select
91	            id={GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_EFFORT_SETTING}
92	            ariaLabel={t("settings.commitAgent.effort")}
93	            value={commitWorker.effort}
94	            options={effortOptions}
95	            onValueChange={(value) => setSetting(GIT_COMMIT_WORKER_EFFORT_SETTING, value)}
96	          />
97	        </div>
98	      </div>
99	    </div>
100	  );
101	}
102

2	import path from "path";
3	import { errorResponse } from "@/server/api-errors";

8	
9	function getAllowedRoot() {
10	  return path.resolve(process.cwd(), "..");
11	}
12	
13	class InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError extends Error {
14	  constructor(message: string) {
15	    super(message);
16	    this.name = "InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError";
17	  }
18	}
19	
20	function getDirectoryCreationErrorStatus(error: unknown) {
21	  const code = error && typeof error === "object" && "code" in error
22	    ? error.code
23	    : null;
24	  if (code === "EEXIST") return 409;
25	  if (code === "ENOENT") return 404;
26	  if (code === "EACCES" || code === "EPERM") return 403;
27	  if (error instanceof InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError || error instanceof SyntaxError) return 400;
28	  return 500;
29	}
30	
31	function resolveInsideAllowedRoot(rawPath: string | null) {
32	  const rootPath = getAllowedRoot();
33	  const resolvedPath = path.resolve(rawPath || rootPath);
34	  return isPathInside(rootPath, resolvedPath) ? resolvedPath : rootPath;
35	}
36	
37	function resolveDirectoryCreation(parentPath: unknown, name: unknown) {
38	  if (typeof parentPath !== "string" || !parentPath.trim()) {
39	    throw new InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError("Parent folder is required.");
40	  }
41	  if (typeof name !== "string") {
42	    throw new InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError("Folder name is required.");
43	  }
44	
45	  const normalizedName = name.trim();
46	  if (
47	    !normalizedName
48	    || normalizedName === "."
49	    || normalizedName === ".."
50	    || normalizedName.includes("/")
51	    || normalizedName.includes("\\")
52	    || normalizedName.includes("\0")
53	    || path.isAbsolute(normalizedName)
54	  ) {
55	    throw new InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError("Folder name must be a single folder name without path separators.");
56	  }
57	
58	  const rootPath = getAllowedRoot();
59	  const resolvedParentPath = path.resolve(parentPath);
60	  if (!isPathInside(rootPath, resolvedParentPath)) {
61	    throw new InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError("Parent folder is outside the allowed filesystem root.");
62	  }
63	
64	  const realRootPath = fs.realpathSync(rootPath);
65	  const realParentPath = fs.realpathSync(resolvedParentPath);
66	  if (!isPathInside(realRootPath, realParentPath) || !fs.statSync(realParentPath).isDirectory()) {
67	    throw new InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError("Parent folder is outside the allowed filesystem root.");
68	  }
69	
70	  // Use the canonical parent so a pre-existing symlink cannot redirect the
71	  // mutation outside the browse root. Node has no portable mkdirat/openat2;
72	  // a same-account actor swapping canonical ancestors between these syscalls
73	  // already has equivalent direct filesystem authority outside this API.
74	  return {
75	    parentPath: realParentPath,
76	    targetPath: path.join(realParentPath, normalizedName),
77	  };
78	}
79	
80	export const handleBrowseFilesystemRequest: OmniHttpHandler = async (request) => {
81	  try {
82	    if (request.method !== "GET") {
83	      return Response.json({ error: { code: "method_not_allowed", message: "Method not allowed." } }, {
84	        status: 405,
85	        headers: { allow: "GET" },
86	      });
87	    }
88	
89	    const auth = await requireApiSession(request, {
90	      source: "Filesystem",
91	      action: "Browse directories",
92	    });
93	    if (auth.response) {
94	      return auth.response;
95	    }
96	
97	    const url = new URL(request.url);
98	    const rootPath = getAllowedRoot();
99	    const dirPath = resolveInsideAllowedRoot(url.searchParams.get("path"));
100	
101	    const entries = fs.readdirSync(dirPath, { withFileTypes: true });
102	    const directories = entries
103	      .filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory() && !entry.name.startsWith("."))
104	      .map((entry) => ({ name: entry.name, path: path.join(dirPath, entry.name) }))
105	      .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
106	
107	    const parentPath = path.dirname(dirPath);
108	    const parent = isPathInside(rootPath, parentPath) ? parentPath : dirPath;
109	
110	    return Response.json({
111	      current: dirPath,
112	      parent,
113	      directories,
114	    });
115	  } catch (error) {
116	    return errorResponse(error, {
117	      status: 400,
118	      source: "Filesystem",
119	      action: "Browse directories",
120	    });
121	  }
122	};
123	
124	export const handleCreateDirectoryRequest: OmniHttpHandler = async (request) => {
125	  let parentPath: string | null = null;
126	  let targetPath: string | null = null;
127	  try {
128	    if (request.method !== "POST") {
129	      return Response.json({ error: { code: "method_not_allowed", message: "Method not allowed." } }, {
130	        status: 405,
131	        headers: { allow: "POST" },
132	      });
133	    }
134	
135	    const auth = await requireApiSession(request, {
136	      source: "Filesystem",
137	      action: "Create folder",
138	      enforceSameOrigin: true,
139	    });
140	    if (auth.response) {
141	      return auth.response;
142	    }
143	
144	    const body = await request.json() as { parentPath?: unknown; name?: unknown };
145	    const resolved = resolveDirectoryCreation(body.parentPath, body.name);
146	    parentPath = resolved.parentPath;
147	    targetPath = resolved.targetPath;
148	    fs.mkdirSync(targetPath);
149	    emitNamedEvent({
150	      kind: "filesystem.directory_created",
151	      parentPath,
152	      path: targetPath,
153	    });
154	
155	    return Response.json({ path: targetPath }, { status: 201 });
156	  } catch (error) {
157	    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
158	    emitNamedEvent({
159	      kind: "filesystem.directory_create_failed",
160	      parentPath,
161	      path: targetPath,
162	      reason: message,
163	    });
164	    emitNamedEvent({
165	      kind: "error.surfaced",
166	      code: "filesystem.directory_create_failed",
167	      message,
168	      surface: "toast",
169	      path: targetPath ?? parentPath ?? undefined,
170	      cause: error instanceof Error ? { name: error.name, message: error.message } : null,
171	    });
172	    return errorResponse(error, {
173	      status: getDirectoryCreationErrorStatus(error),
174	      source: "Filesystem",
175	      action: "Create folder",
176	    });
177	  }
178	};
179	
180	export const handleProjectFilesRequest: OmniHttpHandler = async (request) => {
181	  let action = "Load project files";
182	  try {
183	    if (request.method !== "GET") {
184	      return Response.json({ error: { code: "method_not_allowed", message: "Method not allowed." } }, {
185	        status: 405,
186	        headers: { allow: "GET" },
187	      });
188	    }
189	
190	    const auth = await requireApiSession(request, {
191	      source: "Filesystem",
192	      action: "Load project files",
193	    });
194	    if (auth.response) {
195	      return auth.response;
196	    }
197

202	      action = "Read project file";

204	    }
205	
206	    return Response.json({
207	      root: projectPath,

209	    });
210	  } catch (error) {
211	    return errorResponse(error, {
212	      status: 400,
213	      source: "Filesystem",
214	      action,
215	    });
216	  }
217	};

159	                  type="submit"
160	                  size="sm"
161	                  disabled={!newFolderName.trim() || createDirectory.isPending}
162	                  className="shrink-0"
163	                >
164	                  {createDirectory.isPending ? (
165	                    <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
166	                  ) : null}
167	                  {t(createDirectory.isPending ? "folder.picker.creating" : "folder.picker.create")}
168	                </Button>
169	                <Button
170	                  type="button"
171	                  variant="ghost"
172	                  size="sm"
173	                  onClick={handleCancelDirectoryCreation}
174	                  disabled={createDirectory.isPending}
175	                >
176	                  {t("folder.picker.cancel")}
177	                </Button>
178	              </div>
179	              {createDirectory.error ? (
180	                <div id="folder-picker-create-error" role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">
181	                  {normalizeAppError(createDirectory.error).message}
182	                </div>
183	              ) : null}
184	            </form>
185	          ) : null}
186	          {error ? (
187	            <div className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 p-3 text-sm">
188	              <div className="font-semibold text-destructive">{t("folder.picker.errorTitle")}</div>
189	              <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-foreground">{normalizeAppError(error).message}</div>
190	            </div>
191	          ) : null}
192	          {data && (
193	            <div className="space-y-1">
194	              {data.parent && data.parent !== data.current && (
195	                <Button
196	                  variant="ghost"
197	                  className="w-full justify-start h-8 px-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground"
198	                  onClick={() => handleNavigate(data.parent)}
199	                  >
200	                  <ArrowUpCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" /> ..
201	                </Button>
202	              )}
203	              {directories.map((dir) => (
204	                 <Button
205	                   key={dir.path}
206	                   data-folder-path={dir.path}
207	                  variant="ghost"
208	                  className="w-full justify-start h-8 px-2 text-sm truncate"
209	                  onClick={() => handleNavigate(dir.path)}
210	                >
211	                  <Folder className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 text-primary/70" /> {dir.name}
212	                </Button>
213	              ))}
214	            </div>
215	          )}
216	        </ScrollArea>
217	        
218	        <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-col items-stretch gap-2 border-t bg-muted/20 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
219	          <Button
220	            variant="outline"
221	            size="sm"
222	            onClick={handleStartDirectoryCreation}
223	            disabled={!data?.current || isCreatingDirectory}
224	          >
225	            <FolderPlus className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
226	            {t("folder.picker.newFolder")}
227	          </Button>
228	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:flex sm:items-center">
229	            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => handleOpenChange(false)}>{t("folder.picker.cancel")}</Button>
230	            <Button
231	              size="sm"
232	              onClick={handleSelect}
233	              disabled={!data?.current || createDirectory.isPending}
234	            >
235	              {t("folder.picker.select")}
236	            </Button>
237	          </div>
238	        </div>
239	      </DialogContent>
240	    </Dialog>
241	  );
242	}

1	import fs from "fs";
2	import path from "path";
3	
4	const IGNORED_DIRECTORIES = new Set([
5	  ".git",
6	  ".hg",
7	  ".svn",
8	  ".next",
9	  ".turbo",
10	  ".cache",
11	  "node_modules",
12	  "__pycache__",
13	  "dist",
14	  "build",
15	  "coverage",
16	  "target",
17	  "out",
18	]);
19	
20	const DEFAULT_TEXT_FILE_MAX_BYTES = 512 * 1024;
21	
22	export type ProjectFileContent = {
23	  root: string;
24	  path: string;
25	  content: string;
26	  size: number;
27	  truncated: boolean;
28	};
29	
30	export type ReadProjectTextFileOptions = {
31	  maxBytes?: number;
32	};
33	
34	export function isPathInside(root: string, candidate: string) {
35	  const relative = path.relative(root, candidate);
36	  return relative === "" || (!relative.startsWith("..") && !path.isAbsolute(relative));
37	}
38	
39	function walkFiles(root: string, currentDir: string, files: string[]) {
40	  const entries = fs.readdirSync(currentDir, { withFileTypes: true });
41	
42	  for (const entry of entries) {
43	    if (entry.name.startsWith(".") && entry.name !== ".env") {
44	      continue;
45	    }
46	
47	    const absolutePath = path.join(currentDir, entry.name);
48	    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
49	      if (IGNORED_DIRECTORIES.has(entry.name)) {
50	        continue;
51	      }
52	      walkFiles(root, absolutePath, files);
53	      continue;
54	    }
55	
56	    if (!entry.isFile()) {
57	      continue;
58	    }
59	
60	    files.push(path.relative(root, absolutePath));
61	  }
62	}
63	
64	export function listProjectFiles(root: string) {
65	  const resolvedRoot = path.resolve(root);
66	  const files: string[] = [];
67	  walkFiles(resolvedRoot, resolvedRoot, files);
68	  return files.sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right));
69	}
70	
71	function looksBinary(buffer: Buffer) {
72	  if (buffer.includes(0)) {
73	    return true;
74	  }
75	
76	  const text = buffer.toString("utf8");
77	  if (!text.includes("\uFFFD")) {
78	    return false;
79	  }
80	
81	  const replacementCount = Array.from(text).filter((char) => char === "\uFFFD").length;
82	  return replacementCount > Math.max(2, text.length * 0.01);
83	}
84	
85	export function readProjectTextFile(
86	  root: string,
87	  relativePath: string,
88	  options: ReadProjectTextFileOptions = {},
89	): ProjectFileContent {
90	  const resolvedRoot = path.resolve(root);
91	  const normalizedRelativePath = relativePath.replace(/\\/g, path.sep);
92	  const absolutePath = path.resolve(resolvedRoot, normalizedRelativePath);
93	  if (!isPathInside(resolvedRoot, absolutePath)) {
94	    throw new Error("File path is outside the project root.");
95	  }
96	
97	  const stat = fs.statSync(absolutePath);
98	  if (!stat.isFile()) {
99	    throw new Error("Project path is not a file.");
100	  }
101	
102	  const maxBytes = Math.max(1, options.maxBytes ?? DEFAULT_TEXT_FILE_MAX_BYTES);
103	  const file = fs.openSync(absolutePath, "r");
104	  try {
105	    const readLength = Math.min(stat.size, maxBytes + 1);
106	    const buffer = Buffer.alloc(readLength);
107	    const bytesRead = fs.readSync(file, buffer, 0, readLength, 0);
108	    const readBuffer = buffer.subarray(0, bytesRead);
109	    if (looksBinary(readBuffer)) {
110	      throw new Error("Project file appears to be binary and cannot be displayed.");
111	    }
112	
113	    const truncated = stat.size > maxBytes;
114	    const contentBuffer = truncated ? readBuffer.subarray(0, maxBytes) : readBuffer;
115	    return {
116	      root: resolvedRoot,
117	      path: path.relative(resolvedRoot, absolutePath).replace(/\\/g, "/"),
118	      content: contentBuffer.toString("utf8"),
119	      size: stat.size,
120	      truncated,
121	    };
122	  } finally {
123	    fs.closeSync(file);
124	  }
125	}
126

483	export const handleRunPostRequest: OmniHttpHandler = async (request, context) => {
484	  const probe = startSlowProbe(`POST /api/runs/${context.params?.id ?? "?"}`);
485	  let postActionLabel = "Recover conversation";
486	  try {
487	    if (request.method !== "POST") {
488	      probe.end();
489	      return Response.json({ error: { code: "method_not_allowed", message: "Method not allowed." } }, {
490	        status: 405,
491	        headers: { allow: "POST" },
492	      });
493	    }
494	
495	    const auth = await requireApiSession(request, {
496	      source: "Runs",
497	      action: "Recover conversation",
498	      enforceSameOrigin: true,
499	    });
500	    probe.mark("auth");
501	    if (auth.response) {
502	      probe.end();
503	      return auth.response;
504	    }
505	
506	    const runId = requireRunId(context);
507	    const body = await request.json();
508	    probe.mark("body");
509	    const action = body?.action;
510	    postActionLabel = actionLabelForPostAction(action);
511	    const actionRun = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
512	    probe.mark(`q.runLookup[action=${String(action ?? "?")}]`);
513	    if (!actionRun) {
514	      return errorResponse("Run not found", {
515	        status: 404,
516	        source: "Runs",
517	        action: postActionLabel,
518	      });
519	    }
520	    if (action === "mark_read") {
521	      const lastReadAt = await markRunRead(runId, actionRun);
522	      return Response.json({ ok: true, runId, lastReadAt });
523	    }
524	
525	    const sessionType = normalizeSessionType(actionRun.sessionType);
526	    if (sessionType === "process" && action !== "archive") {

## Claude subagent agent-a8633b02964c1214c: Claude · 2026-08-26T22:37:53.474Z

```text
Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive picture. Let me compile my findings:

## Comprehensive Report: Root Folder / Project Directory Restriction

I've located all the key components that implement the root folder restriction mechanism in OmniHarness. Here's the detailed breakdown:

### (A) WHERE THE ROOT IS CONFIGURED/READ

**1. PROJECTS Setting Storage:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/settings/read.ts` (lines 31-78)
  - `readSettingsState()` function reads the PROJECTS setting from the database
  - Line 33: Fetches the "PROJECTS" setting key
  - Line 35: Calls `canonicalizePersistedProjectRoots()` after reading

**2. PROJECTS Setting API Endpoint:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/http/routes/settings.ts` (lines 137-175)
  - Lines 137-139: Extracts `projectSettingValue` from request body
  - Lines 157-158: After POST, calls `canonicalizePersistedProjectRoots(projectSettingValue)`

**3. Frontend Setting Display:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/HomeApp.tsx` (line 490)
  - `const explicitProjects = useMemo(() => parseProjectList(apiKeys.PROJECTS), [apiKeys.PROJECTS]);`
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/utils.ts` (lines 971-982)
  - `parseProjectList()` parses JSON array of project paths from the PROJECTS setting

**4. Frontend Bootstrap Data:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/bootstrap.ts` (lines 45-100)
  - `buildRuntimeBootstrap()` includes settings via `readSettingsState()`
  - Settings (including PROJECTS) are sent to browser on page load

### (B) EVERY PLACE IT IS ENFORCED/VALIDATED

**1. Move Run to Project Validation:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/http/routes/runs.ts` (lines 426-455)
  - Lines 444-445: Reads PROJECTS setting
  - Lines 446-451: Validates that the projectPath is in `configuredProjectPaths` array
  - Returns 400 error if project is not configured

**2. Project Path Canonicalization:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/projects/canonicalize.ts` (lines 40-87)
  - `canonicalizePersistedProjectRoots()` validates all stored project paths
  - Lines 41-42: Normalizes explicit projects from PROJECTS setting
  - Lines 47-51: Resolves stale project fallbacks using `resolveStaleProjectFallback()`
  - Lines 53-86: For each run, validates/rebases its projectPath against allowed projects

**3. Project Path Matching Logic:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/lib/project-paths.ts` (lines 9-103)
  - `isSameOrDescendantPath()` (lines 9-17): Checks if path is within root
  - `normalizeExplicitProjectPaths()` (lines 19-25): Normalizes and deduplicates project list
  - `resolveStoredProjectRoot()` (lines 69-103): Matches stored project path against allowed projects
  - `resolveStaleProjectFallback()` (lines 27-67): Resolves projects that don't match any allowed root

**4. Directory Browsing Root Constraint:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/http/routes/filesystem.ts` (lines 9-122)
  - Line 9-11: `getAllowedRoot()` returns `path.resolve(process.cwd(), "..")` - one level up from app
  - Lines 31-35: `resolveInsideAllowedRoot()` constrains all browsing to stay within root
  - Lines 98-114: Browse endpoint enforces the root constraint
  - Lines 58-78: Directory creation enforces root constraint with symlink protection

**5. File Operations Safety:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/fs/files.ts` (lines 34-125)
  - Line 34-37: `isPathInside()` helper to validate paths
  - Lines 85-95: `readProjectTextFile()` validates file is within project root before reading

### (C) THE DIRECTORY BROWSING/LISTING API

**1. Browse Endpoint:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/http/routes/filesystem.ts` (lines 80-122)
  - Handler: `handleBrowseFilesystemRequest`
  - Method: GET `/api/fs`
  - Line 99: Query param `path` specifies current directory
  - Lines 101-105: Lists only subdirectories (filters out dotfiles)
  - Returns: `{ current: dirPath, parent: parentPath, directories: [...] }`

**2. Create Directory Endpoint:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/http/routes/filesystem.ts` (lines 124-178)
  - Handler: `handleCreateDirectoryRequest`
  - Method: POST `/api/fs/directories`
  - Lines 144-148: Validates folder name and parent path
  - Lines 58-78: `resolveDirectoryCreation()` enforces root constraint
  - Lines 64-68: Uses `fs.realpathSync()` to resolve symlinks and verify canonical path

**3. Project Files Listing:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/http/routes/filesystem.ts` (lines 180-217)
  - Handler: `handleProjectFilesRequest`
  - Method: GET `/api/fs/files`
  - Line 199: Constrains to allowed root via `resolveInsideAllowedRoot()`
  - Returns list of project files and directory content

**4. Runtime API Wrapper:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime-api/domains/index.ts` (lines 286-298)
  - `files.browse(input = {}, options)` → GET `/api/fs?path={path}`
  - `files.createDirectory(input, options)` → POST `/api/fs/directories`
  - `files.list(input, options)` → GET `/api/fs/files`

### (D) FRONTEND PROJECT-ADD UI

**1. Folder Picker Dialog Component:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/FolderPickerDialog.tsx` (lines 17-242)
  - Component accepts `open`, `onOpenChange`, and `onSelect` props
  - Lines 40-50: Query hook calls `runtimeApis.files.browse({ path: currentPath })`
  - Lines 52-64: Mutation for `runtimeApis.files.createDirectory()`
  - Lines 73-76: `handleNavigate()` moves between directories
  - Lines 78-83: `handleStartDirectoryCreation()` allows creating new folders
  - Lines 105-109: `handleSelect()` calls `onSelect(data.current)` with chosen path
  - UI includes: folder search, navigation, create folder form, directory listing

**2. Add Project Button (Sidebar):**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/ConversationSidebar.tsx` (lines 1100-1115)
  - Line 1107: `onClick={openFolderPicker}` triggers folder picker
  - Icon: `<FolderPlus />`
  - Label i18n key: `conversation.sidebar.addProject`

**3. Integration in HomeApp:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/HomeApp.tsx` (lines 1352, 1729-1733)
  - Line 1352: `const openFolderPicker = useCallback(() => setShowFolderPicker(true), ...)`
  - Lines 1729-1733: FolderPickerDialog rendered with:
    - `open={showFolderPicker}`
    - `onSelect={actions.handleAddProject}` ← calls project add action
  - Line 99-101: Lazy loads FolderPickerDialog component

**4. Add Project Handler:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/useConversationActions.ts` (lines 96-103)
  - `handleAddProject(newPath: string)`:
    - Line 97: Checks if `newPath` not already in `explicitProjects`
    - Line 98: Creates new array with appended path
    - Line 99: Updates apiKeys with new PROJECTS JSON
    - Line 101: Calls `runtimeApis.settings.save(updatedKeys)` to persist

**5. Move Run to Project UI:**
- **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/home/ConversationSidebar.tsx` (lines 672-695)
  - Shows dropdown of available projects from `moveRunToProjectOptions` prop
  - This prop comes from `explicitProjects` array
- **Validation before mutation:**
  - **File:** `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/interface/home/useConversationActions.ts` (lines 204-217)
    - Line 208: Validates `explicitProjects.includes(projectPath)` before submitting move
    - Returns early if project not in allowed list

---

## Summary

The root folder restriction works as follows:

1. **Configuration:** A JSON array of project paths is stored in the `PROJECTS` database setting
2. **Frontend UI:** Users browse directories via `FolderPickerDialog` → constrained by `/api/fs` API
3. **Backend Browsing Root:** Filesystem API limits all browsing to one level above the app (`process.cwd()/../`)
4. **Project Validation:** On move-to-project (PATCH `/api/runs/{id}`), the backend validates the target path exists in PROJECTS setting
5. **Path Matching:** `isSameOrDescendantPath()` in `project-paths.ts` validates paths are within allowed roots
6. **Symlink Protection:** Directory creation uses `fs.realpathSync()` to resolve canonical paths before mutation

24	  if (code === "EEXIST") return 409;

37	function resolveDirectoryCreation(parentPath: unknown, name: unknown) {

39	    throw new InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError("Parent folder is required.");
40	  }
41	  if (typeof name !== "string") {
42	    throw new InvalidDirectoryCreationRequestError("Folder name is required.");
43	  }
44	
45	  const normalizedName = name.trim();

51	    || normalizedName.includes("\\")

58	  const rootPath = getAllowedRoot();
59	  const resolvedParentPath = path.resolve(parentPath);
60	  if (!isPathInside(rootPath, resolvedParentPath)) {

64	  const realRootPath = fs.realpathSync(rootPath);

<!-- codex-event:{"kind":"reasoning","timestamp":"2026-08-26T22:38:14.351Z","phase":null} -->
## Claude thinking · 2026-08-26T22:38:14.351Z

The real blocker is that the allowed root only resolves to the parent of the current working directory, which excludes `/Volumes/Lexar/NLP`. I'm thinking the right fix is to support multiple allowed roots configured via an environment variable, preserving the containment guarantee since this endpoint is effectively a filesystem-read surface behind a session guard.

I'm weighing whether to also union in already-configured project paths so anything added becomes browsable, but that creates a chicken-and-egg problem for adding new projects in the first place unless the picker supports typing a path directly. I also need to check how path resolution and parent-directory logic should work across multiple roots, whether the picker UI needs a root switcher, and how the existing path-containment check handles case sensitivity and trailing separators before deciding on the full implementation.
